    "addedLines": [
      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"
    ],

{
  "parentUuid": "5a2e2afe-9f00-4dd5-9852-4dbfd2987751",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_017kRTB3jF3gh8fY9uF5c98k`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	name: on-experiment-failed
3	description: 实验失败后的自动诊断工作流——查日志分析原因、更新 registry、通知 Director。[CRON] 实验失败消息到达时使用。
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	- workflow
7	- diagnosis
8	- cron-hook
9	tools:
10	- read_registry
11	- tail_training_logs
12	- update_registry
13	- append_idea
14	- feishu_send_result_card
15	- spawn_worker
16	- check_job_status
17	version: 4
18	heavy: true
19	roles:
20	- agent
21	---
22	
23	# 实验失败诊断工作流
24	
25	## 触发方式
26	由 cron hook 自动触发：进程退出且 W&B 无数据 / W&B state=failed/crashed / NaN 检测到。
27	
28	## 步骤
29	
30	### Step 0: 读取项目上下文（诊断前必须执行）
31	先读文档，再看日志——否则诊断方向可能完全错误：
32	
33	- `read_project_file("memory.md")` — **最重要**：查是否有相同失败模式的历史记录和已知修复方案
34	- Worker 通过 `list_repo_tree` + `read_repo_file` 按需从代码仓库读取训练脚本结构
35	
36	读完后检查：
37	- memory.md 里有没有"此类实验曾失败，原因是 X"的记录？→ 直接按已知方案诊断，跳过 Step 2 通用流程
38	- 代码仓库中的训练入口和参数格式是否与本次实验配置匹配？（Worker 可用 `list_repo_tree` + `read_repo_file` 查看）
39	
40	### Step 1: 了解失败背景
41	- read_registry(exp_id) — 查看状态、conclusion、notes、metrics、log_path
42	
43	### Step 2: 浅层日志诊断
44	有 log_path 时：tail_training_logs(server, **绝对路径**, lines=80)
45	- 寻找：OOM、CUDA error、NaN、assertion error、数据加载错误、Python Traceback
46	- 没找到 → 增加行数再查
47	
48	### Step 2.5: 深查（满足以下任一条件时 spawn_worker）
49	- 运行时长 < 5 分钟（初始化阶段崩溃）
50	- tail 日志中未见明确 Traceback 或错误行
51	- 同类实验连续失败 ≥ 2 次
52	
53	```python
54	spawn_worker(
55	    task_type="diagnose:{exp_id}",
56	    task_prompt="""SSH 到服务器 {server}，诊断实验 {exp_id} 的失败原因。
57	
58	项目路径：{project_path}（绝对路径）
59	日志路径：{log_path}（可能为空）
60	
61	执行步骤（按顺序，有结论就停）：
62	
63	1. 如果有日志路径：
64	   - 先看结尾 50 行（找 Traceback / Error / CUDA OOM）
65	   - 没找到明确错误：再看开头 100 行（初始化崩溃在这里）
66	   - 日志文件不存在：说明任务启动即失败，跳到步骤 2
67	
68	2. 日志无错误或无日志时，检查基础设施：
69	   - 入口文件是否存在
70	   - 激活环境并验证 import
71	   - 检查 configs/ 目录
72	
73	3. 检查磁盘/内存（OOM 类）
74	
75	最后输出一行 JSON：
76	{"root_cause":"<引用具体报错行>","error_type":"infrastructure|hyperparameter|resource|data","fix_steps":["步骤1","步骤2"],"can_auto_fix":true}""",
77	    tool_groups=["ssh"],
78	)
79	```
80	
81	Worker 返回后，以其结论作为 Step 3 的诊断依据。
82	
83	### Step 3: 诊断 + 行动
84	根据 `error_type` 决定：
85	- **infrastructure**（文件缺失/import error/配置语法）→ spawn_worker 修复，修复后重提交（自主修复最多 1 次，再失败则停止告知用户）。注意：此重试策略仅适用于训练实验，数据生成任务的重试策略参见 `on-datagen-done` 的 Step 9。
86	  - spawn_worker 后立即：`update_registry(exp_id, "notes", "fix_worker_id: {worker_id}")`，确保关联可追溯
87	- **hyperparameter/架构**（loss 不降、指标差）→ 给具体调整建议，用户决定
88	- **resource**（OOM、GPU 不够）→ 给配置调整建议，用户确认
89	- **data**（路径、格式）→ spawn_worker 检查，能自修则修，不能则告知用户
90	
91	### Step 4: 更新 registry
92	- update_registry(exp_id, "notes", "失败原因: ... | 已有成果: ... | 建议: ...")
93	- update_registry(exp_id, "conclusion", "failed")
94	- update_registry(exp_id, "status", "DONE")
95	
96	### Step 5: 发送飞书结果卡片
97	- feishu_send_result_card(exp_id, conclusion="failed", key_metrics, baseline_comparison)
98	  - key_metrics：从 registry 取已有 metrics，没有则传空 dict
99	  - baseline_comparison：Step 3 的诊断摘要
100	
101	### Step 6: 提炼 insight + 更新文档
102	
103	**写入 memory.md（新失败模式才写，避免重复）**：
104	如果 root_cause 是之前未记录的模式（Step 0 读 memory.md 时没有看到），用 `save_memory` 追加：
105	```
106	- [失败模式] {error_type}：{root_cause 一句话摘要} → 修复方案：{fix_steps 摘要}
107	```
108	例：`- [失败模式] GPU 硬件：GPU 7 CUDA capability 检查失败，device=7, num_gpus=7 → 设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 跳过 GPU 7`
109	这样下次同类实验失败时，Step 0 读 memory.md 就能直接命中，跳过通用诊断流程。
110	
111	**更新 ideas.md（有价值的 insight 才写）**：
112	- AMP 崩溃反复出现 → `append_idea` 记录调参方向
113	- 部分 metrics 有价值但后期崩溃 → `append_idea` 记录调优方向
114	
115	更新 ideas.md IN_PROGRESS → DEAD ENDS：
116	- move_idea(from_section="IN_PROGRESS（进行中）", to_section="DEAD ENDS（已确认死路）", index=N)
117	- 找不到对应条目：跳过
118	
119	### Step 6.5: 更新 report.md
120	
121	report.md 由系统根据 registry 状态自动生成，无需手动调用 `save_project_file`。Step 4 更新 registry 后，report 会自动刷新并在 ## 告警 区块突出标注本次失败。
122	
123	### Step 7: 回复
124	生成失败报告：exp_id、失败原因（引用具体错误行）、已有成果、具体建议。
125	
````

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Memory — faith-evidence-ie\\n\\n## 环境与兼容性\\n\\n- **AutoDL HuggingFace 下载不可靠**：大模型（如 Qwen3-4B）从 HF 下载反复 ReadTimeout。解决方案：用 ModelScope (`from modelscope import snapshot_download`) 下载到本地缓存，再用本地路径加载。(2026-05-14)\\n- **vLLM 版本不兼容 TRL**：服务器预装 vLLM 0.18.1，但 TRL 支持范围为 0.11.0-0.17.1。MVP-2 若用 TRL 内部 vLLM rollout 可能有兼容问题，需提前测试。(2026-05-14)\\n- **flash_attn 未安装**：rtx5090-4x 服务器未预装 flash-attn，训练脚本需用 `attn_implementation=\\\"sdpa\\\"` 替代。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Qwen3-4B 本地路径**：已通过 ModelScope 下载到 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B`（7.6GB）。后续训练脚本应指向此路径而非 HF model ID。(2026-05-14)\\n- **确认训练配置**：ZeRO-2 + QLoRA (4-bit NF4, rank 64, alpha 128) + SDPA。ZeRO-3 + QLoRA 不兼容（uint8 vs all_gather dtype）。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Smoke test 假阴性**：synthetic data reward variance=0 导致零梯度是数学必然（format reward 对所有 completions 输出相同分数），不是训练 bug。评估 smoke test 时只看兼容性和显存，不看 loss 趋势。(2026-05-14)\\n- **TRL 1.1.0 API 变更（关键）**：服务器 TRL 版本 1.1.0，与旧版有 3 个破坏性变更：(1) `SFTConfig.max_seq_length` → `max_length`；(2) `SFTTrainer(tokenizer=...)` → `SFTTrainer(processing_class=...)`；(3) 多 GPU 需要 accelerate `MULTI_GPU` + `device_map={\\\"\\\": local_rank}`。**grpo_trainer.py 也用了 `tokenizer=`，GRPO 提交前必须改为 `processing_class=`**。(2026-05-14)\\n- **rtx5090-4x 和 gpu-5090-4x-1 是同一台机器**：两个 SSH endpoint 指向同一台 4×RTX 5090 32GB 服务器。三路 GRPO 对比必须串行执行，不能并行。串行总时间 ~18-19h。(2026-05-14)\\n\\n## GRPO 训练经验\\n\\n- **Format reward hacking 是 GRPO 的首要风险**：CED r1 mode collapse 根因——模型学会输出短空 JSON 拿 format≈1.0，F1 归零。format reward 占 total 的 60-70% 时会主导优化方向。修复：(1) 加 KL penalty (kl_coef≥0.05) 约束累积漂移；(2) format reward 降权（×0.3）或改 binary gate；(3) 加 save_steps 保留中间 checkpoint。(2026-05-14)\\n- **clip_ratio=0 在 QLoRA GRPO 中可能是正常的**：QLoRA 可训练参数少，单步 policy ratio 变化 < ε=0.2，PPO clip 不触发。TRL GRPO `num_iterations=1` 时 clip 机制天然不生效（只一次迭代，ratio 恒≈1.0）。clip_ratio=0 本身不是 bug，但意味着必须用 KL penalty 作为替代约束。(2026-05-14)\\n- **save_strategy=\\\"epoch\\\" 对长 epoch 是灾难**：189 steps/epoch 意味着跑几小时没有任何 checkpoint，collapse 时无法 resume。GRPO 训练必须用 save_steps（推荐 20）。(2026-05-14)\\n\", \"total_chars\": 1928, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

```text
Now I can see the full picture. Let me analyze:

1. The training ran with `--stage 3` (CED reward) and `--num_iterations 1` and `--kl_coef 0.05` and `--max_kl 100`
2. After step 5 (out of 756), KL divergence exploded to 4,449,719.63 (threshold 100.0), and training was stopped
3. Then it seems like the process did a second run (perhaps it ran eval or a continuation?), and KL exploded even more to 69,496,242,176.01


4. The process exited with code 129 (SIGHUP signal)

Looking at the logs more carefully, there appear to be two separate training runs both stopping at step 5 with wildly different KL values. The timestamps suggest they happened around the same time, which is odd for a single-card training setup. The command was running a GRPO trainer, so either there's logging interleaving from multiple processes or something else is going on with how the training was initiated.

The progress bars show two concurrent instances with slightly different step timings, both hitting KL divergence issues at step 5. Given the command specified `--num_iterations 1`, I'm wondering if the training was accidentally submitted twice or if there's something about how TRL GRPO handles iterations that's causing this duplication.

The real issue is clear though: KL divergence exploded catastrophically to 4.4M at step 5, far exceeding the threshold of 100. This triggered the KL callback to halt training and save the model. The EXIT_CODE=129 (SIGHUP) is puzzling since the logs show the training completed gracefully with "GRPO training (stage 3) complete," so either the KL callback stopped it cleanly and then the process received SIGHUP, or the tmux session itself got interrupted.

This is the third consecutive failure for CED GRPO — the pattern shows mode collapse in the first run, and now massive KL divergence in subsequent runs, indicating the model's policy is diverging severely from the reference policy.

Looking at the metrics from step 5, the loss exploded to nearly 270k while KL divergence hit 4.4 million, far exceeding the 100 threshold. The entropy collapsed to 0.084, meaning the model became overconfident and peaked, while rewards remained low across all metrics. The clip ratio stayed at zero, so PPO clipping never engaged.

The core issue appears to be that `kl_coef=0.05` provides insufficient regularization — with only a single iteration per step, the policy can shift dramatically without any clipping constraint to rein it in, and the weak KL penalty can't prevent this divergence.

But examining the gradient norm at 0.15625 rules out a gradient explosion, and the model is still generating completions of reasonable length. That loss value of 269987 is suspiciously high though, which makes me wonder if there's a problem with how the reference model was set up after merging the SFT adapter into the base and then creating a fresh LoRA for GRPO training.

The real culprit might be the 4-bit quantization rounding errors introduced during the merge—those could be causing the initial KL divergence between the GRPO policy and reference model to be nonzero from the start, then compounding rapidly. Combined with a kl_coef of only 0.05 being too weak to rein it in and a learning rate of 5e-5 that's probably too aggressive for single-GPU training without gradient accumulation, I'm looking at a hyperparameter and architecture mismatch rather than a training instability.

The KL explodes within 5 steps, hitting 4.4M when the threshold is 100—so I need to increase the KL coefficient, lower the learning rate, and add gradient accumulation steps to stabilize training. I'll categorize this as a hyperparameter issue and walk through the diagnostic steps to give the user concrete suggestions they can adjust.

Now I need to update the registry and send the results to Feishu, then update the memory file with this failure mode. I also notice there's a separate `mvp2_grpo_extract` experiment in ANALYZING status that came through an `on_complete` event, but the current message is focused on `mvp2_grpo_ced_r3`, so I'll handle that one first and check the logs more carefully to understand what happened across the experiment's iterations.

Looking at the r3 logs, I see the KL divergence exploded dramatically—starting from the initial state, jumping to 4.4M in the first pass and then to 69.5B in the second pass. The early stopping mechanism correctly caught this divergence and halted training, but because no meaningful training occurred, the experiment failed with EXIT_CODE=129, which appears to be a side effect of how the early stop mechanism handles exit codes. This is now the third consecutive CED GRPO failure, so I need to investigate the pattern and update my tracking accordingly.

The failures show a clear progression: r1 had mode collapse from reward hacking, r2 hit OOM with multi-GPU setup, and r3 shows KL explosion within just 5 steps. The core problem is that GRPO training is fundamentally unstable for this task. I should consider several stabilization approaches: reducing the learning rate substantially (to 1e-6 or 5e-6), adding gradient accumulation to smooth out updates, increasing the KL coefficient to more aggressively penalize divergence, introducing warmup steps to gradually ramp up policy gradients, and investigating whether the QLoRA merge rounding is introducing a fundamental instability. Let me compare the r3 command against r1 to see what changed.

The key difference is that r1 used 4 GPUs with gradient accumulation for an effective batch size of 64, while r3 runs on a single GPU with batch size 1 and no accumulation—this creates extremely noisy gradients that immediately destabilize the KL divergence. I need to either restore the multi-GPU setup with proper gradient accumulation or find a way to stabilize single-GPU training through much more conservative hyperparameters. The extract and flatnli runs also lack gradient accumulation, but they're using different reward weights—extract disables CED rewards entirely while flatnli enables them at full strength, which might explain why r3 with CED is hitting instability that the others aren't.

Looking at the actual KL values, the second training pass exploded to 69.5 billion compared to the first pass's 4.4 million, suggesting it loaded from an already-diverged checkpoint and cascaded from there. The log shows stage 3 completed after an early stop, then another training kicked off—I'm wondering if there's retry logic in the trainer or if something else is triggering a second pass.

Rather than getting lost in the implementation details, the core issue is clear: KL explosion during stage 3, which points to a hyperparameter problem. I should report this finding and move forward with the skill steps.

Now I'm updating the registry with the diagnosis, preparing the Feishu result card, and notifying the Director. Since hyperparameter tuning requires user input per the skill guidelines, I'll recommend adjustments and wait for their decision before resubmitting. I also notice the report.md needs updating to reflect the current experiment status, though it should auto-refresh once I update the registry.

Looking at resource allocation, cuda:0 is now free after r3 failed, and cuda:1 is still running mvp2_sft_eval. I should also check on mvp2_grpo_extract which is in ANALYZING state—that'll likely come through as a separate callback, so I'll leave it for now and focus on the r3 report.

环境与兼容性\\n\\n- **AutoDL HuggingFace 下载不可靠**：大模型（如 Qwen3-4B）从 HF 下载反复 ReadTimeout。解决方案：用 ModelScope (`from modelscope import snapshot_download`) 下载到本地缓存，再用本地路径加载。(2026-05-14)\\n- **vLLM 版本不兼容 TRL**：服务器预装 vLLM 0.18.1，但 TRL 支持范围为 0.11.0-0.17.1。MVP-2 若用 TRL 内部 vLLM rollout 可能有兼容问题，需提前测试。(2026-05-14)\\n- **flash_attn 未安装**：rtx5090-4x 服务器未预装 flash-attn，训练脚本需用 `attn_implementation=\\\"sdpa\\\"` 替代。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Qwen3-4B 本地路径**：已通过 ModelScope 下载到 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B`（7.6GB）。后续训练脚本应指向此路径而非 HF model ID。(2026-05-14)\\n- **确认训练配置**：ZeRO-2 + QLoRA (4-bit NF4, rank 64, alpha 128) + SDPA。ZeRO-3 + QLoRA 不兼容（uint8 vs all_gather dtype）。(2026-05-14)\\n- **Smoke test 假阴性**：synthetic data reward variance=0 导致零梯度是数学必然（format reward 对所有 completions 输出相同分数），不是训练 bug。评估 smoke test 时只看兼容性和显存，不看 loss 趋势。(2026-05-14)\\n- **TRL 1.1.0 API 变更（关键）**：服务器 TRL 版本 1.1.0，与旧版有 3 个破坏性变更：(1) `SFTConfig.max_seq_length` → `max_length`；(2) `SFTTrainer(tokenizer=...)` → `SFTTrainer(processing_class=...)`；(3) 多 GPU 需要 accelerate `MULTI_GPU` + `device_map={\\\"\\\": local_rank}`。**grpo_trainer.py 也用了 `tokenizer=`，GRPO 提交前必须改为 `processing_class=`**。(2026-05-14)\\n- **rtx5090-4x 和 gpu-5090-4x-1 是同一台机器**：两个 SSH endpoint 指向同一台 4×RTX 5090 32GB 服务器。三路 GRPO 对比必须串行执行，不能并行。串行总时间 ~18-19h。(2026-05-14)\\n\\n## GRPO 训练经验\\n\\n- **Format reward hacking 是 GRPO 的首要风险**：CED r1 mode collapse 根因——模型学会输出短空 JSON 拿 format≈1.0，F1 归零。format reward 占 total 的 60-70% 时会主导优化方向。修复：(1) 加 KL penalty (kl_coef≥0.05) 约束累积漂移；(2) format reward 降权（×0.3）或改 binary gate；(3) 加 save_steps 保留中间 checkpoint。(2026-05-14)\\n- **clip_ratio=0 在 QLoRA GRPO 中可能是正常的**：QLoRA 可训练参数少，单步 policy ratio 变化 < ε=0.2，PPO clip 不触发。TRL GRPO `num_iterations=1` 时 clip 机制天然不生效（只一次迭代，ratio 恒≈1.0）。clip_ratio=0 本身不是 bug，但意味着必须用 KL penalty 作为替代约束。(2026-05-14)\\n- **save_strategy=\\\"epoch\\\" 对长 epoch 是灾难**：189 steps/epoch 意味着跑几小时没有任何 checkpoint，collapse 时无法 resume。GRPO 训练必须用 save_steps（推荐 20）。(2026-05-14)\\n\", \"total_chars\": 1928, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

reward hacking 是 GRPO 的首要风险**：CED r1 mode collapse 根因——模型学会输出短空 JSON 拿 format≈1.0，F1 归零。format reward 占 total 的 60-70% 时会主导优化方向。修复：(1) 加 KL penalty (kl_coef≥0.05) 约束累积漂移；(2) format reward 降权（×0.3）或改 binary gate；(3) 加 save_steps 保留中间 checkpoint。(2026-05-14)\n- **clip_ratio=0 在 QLoRA GRPO 中可能是正常的**：QLoRA 可训练参数少，单步 policy ratio 变化 < ε=0.2，PPO clip 不触发。TRL GRPO `num_iterations=1` 时 clip 机制天然不生效（只一次迭代，ratio 恒≈1.0）。clip_ratio=0 本身不是 bug，但意味着必须用 KL penalty 作为替代约束。(2026-05-14)\n- **save_strategy=\"epoch\" 对长 epoch 是灾难**：189 steps/epoch 意味着跑几小时没有任何 checkpoint，collapse 时无法 resume。GRPO 训练必须用 save_steps（推荐 20）。(2026-05-14)\n- **[失败模式] 单卡 GRPO 无 grad_accum → KL 5 步爆炸**：单 GPU batch_size=1 无 gradient_accumulation_steps，有效 batch=1 导致梯度噪声极高，KL 在 step 5 即达 4.4M（阈值 100）。单卡 GRPO 必须至少 `--gradient_accumulation_steps 16`（有效 batch≥16），并降 lr 至 5e-6，增 kl_coef 至 0.5。QLoRA merge 的量化舍入会放大初始 policy-reference gap，进一步加剧不稳定。(2026-05-14)\n"
}
```

[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办

- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪
